#2165. WATCH: Nico Raskin turns interviewer after Rangers teammate's Scotland joy

James Penrice was welcomed into the Scotland fold with a cheeky message from Rangers team-mate Nico Raskin after earning his first senior international call-up.

The 27-year-old left-back has been selected by Sébastien Pocognoli for this month’s Nations League double-header against North Macedonia and Switzerland.

🏴󠁧󠁢󠁳󠁣󠁴󠁿 A first @ScotlandNT call-up for James Penrice! 💙 James joins teammates Findlay Curtis and Liam Kelly in the squad for the upcoming UEFA Nations League fixtures. 🎙️ Fresh off the training pitch, @RangersTV and Nico Raskin caught up with James following the news. https://t.co/jMukr0qH2n

— Rangers Football Club (@RangersFC) November 3, 2026

Raskin was first to interview his team-mate after the news broke, joking: “Well done James, what do you want me to ask? What is the question? How do you feel to be called up, brother?”

Penrice replied: “Incredibly proud. Buzzing for my family as well.”

Raskin then joked about Belgian boss Pocognoli’s decision to select the Rangers defender. He said: “Well-deserved. I always knew Belgian managers have good eyes to see players!”

Speaking to Rangers TV afterwards, Penrice revealed he had only discovered he was in the squad minutes before heading out for training. He said: “Five minutes before I came out.

“Kev zoomed round in the cart and came right up to me and just went… and I went, ‘Oh my God, unreal!’”

Rangers youth prospect reveals personal ‘privilege’ of working with boyhood hero

Russell Martin ‘spoken highly of’ at Manchester United before Rangers stint

Pedro Caixinha back in Europe after joining 13th club of managerial career

Penrice admitted an international call-up was something he could scarcely have imagined earlier in his career. He added: “I’ve waited a wee while, but I knew coming back to Scotland, coming to Rangers, it would help my cause.

“It’s credit to the boys in there as well. They’ve helped me incredibly, and I’m just buzzing.

“It’s a weird feeling because I didn’t ever think going through the early part of the career, I would ever be called up. But it’s happened now, so I’m just delighted.

“It’s an incredibly proud moment for me and my family.”

#2164. La rete di Pinamonti in Lazio-Cagliari: l'analisi dell'ex arbitro Marco Gabriele

L’ex arbitro Marco Gabriele analizza a livello regolamentare la rete di Andrea Pinamonti contro il Cagliari

L’ex arbitro Marco Gabriele è intervenuto ai microfoni di Radio Radio per esaminare il discusso gol di Andrea Pinamonti, arrivato sul finale di Lazio-Cagliari. L'episodio continua a far discutere poiché la rete del 2-1 è nata mentre Deiola era rimasto a terra dopo una pallonata, con il gioco che è proseguito regolarmente fino alla conclusione vincente dell'attaccante.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

A mio avviso Colombo ha gestito correttamente la situazione e la rete è regolare. Dopo la conclusione di Dele-Bashiru, il pallone è rimasto per alcuni secondi a disposizione dei giocatori del Cagliari, prima che l’azione proseguisse. Questo è un elemento importante nella valutazione dell’episodio.

L’arbitro non è obbligato a interrompere automaticamente il gioco ogni volta che un calciatore viene colpito dal pallone. Lo deve fare quando ritiene che ci sia un infortunio grave. Per questo, secondo la mia valutazione, Colombo ha fatto bene a lasciar proseguire l’azione.

#2163. Calciomercato no stop - Indiscrezioni, trattative e retroscena del 4 novembre

ROMA, TRIS DI RINFORZI PER GASP. BIG D'EUROPA SULLE TRACCE DI GATTUSO. JUVE E ROMA SULLE TRACCE DI WIAFE E DEGLI EMISSARI BIANCONERI ERANO AL MAPEI A VISIONARE BAKOLA

Come l'anno scorso, Gian Piero Gasperini ha un tormentone per il gennaio successivo. Un anno fa era l'attaccante, riuscendo a trovare poi in Donyell Malen il centravanti perfetto, capace di spostare gli equilibri come pochi altri hanno fatto all'arrivo in Serie A, ma anche dopo. La speranza è quella di prendere almeno un giocatore simile. Al netto delle discrete prestazioni di Rodrigo Mora, protagonista anche all'Old Trafford - almeno finché non è finita la benzina - servirà un esterno sinistro con il piede invertito, anche per dare cambi e brillantezza alla fase offensiva. I nomi in estate sono stati diversi, mentre per gennaio potrebbero anche esserci qualche possibilità in prestito e non per forza con un titolo definitivo. Questo anche per dare sollievo alle casse, ma la scelta dovrà essere - almeno sulla carta - di ottimo livello. Poi l'idea di Gasperini è quella di avere altri due giocatori che possano essere delle buone riserve. Un quinto di centrocampo per dare ricambio ai titolari, magari a sinistra dove Wesley e Rensch sono adattati - pur facendo bene - più un mediano che possa dare un'altra alternativa ai titolari. La coperta è attualmente corta anche per gli infortuni, ma con i tanti impegni e la speranza di avere arrivare in fondo alle competizioni c'è l'idea di non lasciare nulla al caso.

Gennaro Gattuso è attualmente il nono allenatore più pagato della Serie A e percepisce 1,2 milioni di euro a stagione. Il suo contratto scadrà nel 2027, con un'opzione legata al 12° posto che farebbe allungare l'accordo fino al 2028. Secondo quanto riportato da Il Messaggero, alcune big d'Europa hanno già cominciato ad allacciare i primi contatti con gli intermediari che lo hanno portato alla Lazio. I biancocelesti dal canto loro non hanno fatto presente al tecnico nient'altro che fiducia. Certo, oggi non potrebbe essere altrimenti, ma è altrettanto vero che la Lazio lo ha scelto quando, dopo la batosta con la Nazionale, non in molti lo avrebbero fatto. Ora il presidente sa che deve blindarlo, resta solo da capire se avrà l'intenzione di procedere con le trattative per un adeguamento o meno, la scelta è sua e le conseguenze che ne deriveranno saranno una responsabilità che dovrà assumersi.

Cinque presenze nelle ultime cinque partite, Samuel Wiafe rischia di diventare un oggetto di mercato. Perché nelle ultime settimane sia la Juventus che la Roma stanno seguendo con particolare intenzione il centrocampista del Genoa. Esordio con la Fiorentina, dal primo minuto con la Roma, ha raccolto altre due partenze fra i titolari. Insomma, la sensazione è di trovarsi di fronte a un giocatore in grande crescita, dopo che nella scorsa stagione faceva il titolare in Primavera 2 con il Modena, rientrato in un affare allargato da 2,5 milioni di euro più Odero e Consiglio in direzione Emilia. Diciotto anni appena compiuti, il futuro di Wiafe verrà deciso nei prossimi mesi. Anche perché non c'è una possibile valutazione, consdierati anche i recenti casi, fra Ekhator ad Ahanor, con cui i rossoblù sono riusciti a mettere a segno plusvalenze importanti con Juventus e Atalanta. Nelle scorse settimane ha ricevuto anche la convocazione in Under 19, con la 21 di Silvio Baldini che ci farà più di un pensierino.

Darryl Bakola è uno dei giovani emergenti più interessanti della Serie A. Coinvolto - come tutti - nelle ultime settimane horror del Sassuolo, queste non cancellano le ottime prestazioni e le grandi caratteristiche fisiche e tecniche mostrare nei primi mesi della sua esperienza in neroverde. Così a Reggio Emilia, per Sassuolo-Fiorentina, era presente un emissario del club bianconero per studiarne i progressi, anche se lo stesso Giovanni Carnevali lo conosce bene. Lo ha pescato lui dall'Olympique Marsiglia, pagandolo non poco nonostante avesse compiuto da poco diciotto anni: 10 milioni di euro lo scorso due febbraio. La pagella per TMW è stata positiva, visto che è stato uno dei pochi a salvarsi con un sei al netto di qualche imprecisione di troppo. "Impatto discreto sul match: provvidenziale la sua chiusura a corpo morto sulla linea a negare il gol a Beto. In mezzo al campo, però, non è sempre preciso". Nelle settimane scorse si è parlato di un valore fra i 25 e i 30 milioni. Ovviamente il fatto che sia un teenager, con un ingaggio molto abbordabile per un club come la Juventus, può essere una buona scelta per i bianconeri, alla ricerca di un'alternativa in mezzo al campo. Da capire se i buoni rapporti fra le parti - basti pensare all'affare Adzic - potranno favorire ulteriormente la buona riuscita dell'affare, in caso la Juve decidesse di affondare.

ZUBIMENDI GIOCA POCO ALL'ARSENAL, CI PENSA IL REAL MADRID. NEL FUTURO DEL BABY TALENTO JJ GABRIEL C'E' IL REAL MADRID, BARCELLONA SUPERATO. IL LIONE ACCATTA LE DIMISSIONI DI FONSECA: DALLO SPORTING 5 MILIONI. IL MONACO CERCA LIQUIDITA' E BALOGUN PUO' SALUTARE A GENNAIO

Il calciomercato invernale è una partita a scacchi dove la pazienza conta più della fretta, lo sa bene il Real Madrid, che per la sessione di riparazione di gennaio ha già tracciato una linea strategica chiara, nessun acquisto impulsivo, rosa confermata e focus sul finale di stagione. Eppure, nel calcio le certezze sono fatte per essere messe alla prova, e il nome di Martín Zubimendi, improvvisamente tornato a orbitare nei radar della Casa Blanca, rappresenta il classico imprevisto capace di far vacillare anche i piani più rigidi. Il centrocampista della nazionale spagnola sta vivendo una fase di profonda riflessione a Londra, arrivato all'Arsenal con lo status di fulcro del centrocampo, le dinamiche interne e le rotazioni tecniche ne stanno limitando l'impiego, aprendo una crepa che a gennaio potrebbe trasformarsi in una clamorosa separazione. Davanti a uno scenario del genere, i grandi club europei si sono mossi nell'ombra, il Real Madrid osserva da una posizione di vigile attesa. Florentino Pérez non ha fretta e non ha ancora avviato alcuna trattativa formale, ma la storia recente del club insegna che le grandi opportunità di mercato si gestiscono così, monitorando ogni minima variazione delle condizioni prima di affondare il colpo.

Secondo quanto riferito dall'esperto di calciomercato Matteo Moretto, il giovane talento del Manchester United JJ Gabriel sarebbe ormai ad un passo dal formalizzare il suo passaggio al Real Madrid. Dopo aver presentato una richiesta formale di cessione ai Red Devils, il prodigio inglese classe 2010 si trova attualmente a Madrid per allenarsi, in attesa che vengano definiti gli ultimi dettagli prima del suo approdo ufficiale tra le fila dei Blancos.

Era nell'aria, ora è ufficiale: Paulo Fonseca ha informato la dirigenza del Lione della propria volontà di rassegnare le dimissioni per accettare l'offerta dello Sporting Portugal, club a cui è particolarmente legato e pronto a versare una penale da 5 milioni di euro per liberarlo. Preso atto della decisione del tecnico portoghese — accolta con profondo rammarico e disappunto per via della tempistica a stagione in corso —, il club francese ha ritenuto inopportuno proseguire un rapporto privo delle necessarie garanzie di totale impegno, avviando immediatamente la ricerca del nuovo allenatore. Nel frattempo, la guida tecnica della prima squadra è stata affidata ad interim a Jorge Maciel, che rimarrà in panchina fino alla prossima sosta per le nazionali. Il traghettatore dirigerà il gruppo già a partire dalla sfida di Europa League contro la Real Sociedad, per poi guidare i compagni nel successivo impegno di campionato sul campo del Brest, mentre la dirigenza lavora a un profilo di continuità in grado di portare avanti i principi e l'ambizioso progetto sportivo sviluppato negli ultimi mesi.

Il Monaco si prepara a un mercato invernale movimentato. Il club del Principato, alle prese con la necessità di riequilibrare i conti, punta soprattutto a una cessione nel reparto offensivo e il nome destinato a lasciare la squadra resta quello di Folarin Balogun. L’attaccante statunitense, arrivato dall’Arsenal per una cifra importante e sotto contratto fino al 2028, non dovrebbe essere trattenuto anche a gennaio. Dopo un avvio di stagione complicato, Balogun sta ritrovando spazio e fiducia: domenica ha segnato il suo primo gol stagionale nella sconfitta contro il Paris FC, ma il suo futuro resta lontano dal Principato. Già in estate il giocatore era stato vicino al trasferimento all’Everton per circa 45 milioni di euro più bonus, prima che l’affare saltasse; il Monaco spera ora di concretizzare una cessione per alleggerire il bilancio e avere maggiore margine sul mercato.

#2162. Rangers game rescheduled as a result of Premier Sports Cup final participation

Rangers' trip to St Johnstone in December has been pushed back due to the Premier Sports Cup final.

#2161. Allegri in conferenza stampa: “Brutto momento della stagione. Occorre lavorare e stare zitti”

Ecco le parole di Massimiliano Allegri dopo il ko del Napoli per 4-1 contro il Porto:

“È un brutto momento non della partita, ma della stagione, abbiamo perso tre partite su quattro in Champions e in campionato siamo indietro. Abbiamo fatto bene per 70 minuti, poi ci sono i cambi, in Champions incidono, fanno la differenza. Occorre lavorare, stare zitti, mettere domenica in campo la stessa determinazione finchè hanno retto. Dopo la Lazio ci sarà la sosta, rientreranno dei giocatori, poi da lì si vedrà, accusare i giocatori o altri non è nel mio carattere e nel mio stile, oggi soprattutto non ho da rimproverare nulla ai ragazzi. Sul primo gol si sono invertiti i marcatori McTominay e Vergara, poi loro sono stati bravi a cambiare e siamo stati poco lucidi ad andare a prendere l’avversario. Mi sembra che la squadra abbia fatto per 70 minuti una buona partita, avevamo una sola chance: andarli a prendere a tutto campo, Vergara si è comportato molto bene, Politano anche. Dovevamo rimanere in partita perchè è un conto perdere 2-1, un altro 4-1. Dopo la sosta recupereremo dei calciatori e vedremo”.

#2158. Marco Verratti, nove volte Campione di Francia che non ha mai giocato in Serie A

Sin dal suo arrivo, quindicenne, nel calcio che conta, Marco Verratti aveva fatto parlare di sé. Giocatore di una qualità superiore e intelligenza calcistica straordinaria, non ha mai giocato in Serie A. Perché dopo la promozione del Pescara di Zdenek Zeman, in compagnia di Lorenzo Insigne e Ciro Immobile, il Paris Saint Germain ha deciso di acquistarlo come primo perno di una squadra che puntava - ma che non ha mai vinto - la Champions League.

Ovviamente il PSG degli sceicchi lo ha portato a vincere molto. Nove volte campione di Francia, sei coppe, altrettante coppe di lega, nove supercoppe francesi. Però la soddisfazione migliore se l'è tirata fuori con la nazionale, vincendo l'Europeo del 2020 a Wembley. Mancini schierava il doppio pivot, con lui e Jorginho che erano i registi: chissà che non possa essere un'opzione anche per questa Italia. Probabilmente non con lui.

L'ultima vera notizia di Marco Verratti era la possibile convocazione con la Bosnia Erzegovina, nel match per la qualificazione agli scorsi Mondiali. Poi però ci fu un infortunio al ginocchio che lo costringe a saltare la tornata di chiamate. Ora è tornato in pianta stabile nell'Al Duhail, dove ha giocato cinque gare di campionato con due assist. Oggi Marco Verratti compie 34 anni.

#2157. Stephen Robinson proud of his battling Dons but injury crisis deepens

Published: 17:30 EDT, 31 October 2026 | Updated: 14:43 EDT, 2 November 2026

Aberdeen boss Stephen Robinson has revealed his injury crisis has become even more catastrophic ahead of the weekend league visit of Hibernian that has the potential to make or break his reign.

The Dons manager saw his side put in a fine, disciplined display in going down 1-0 to Rangers in the Premier Sports Cup final, but lost Emmanuel Gyamfi within 15 minutes after he collided with Kim Min-su and also has Brad Lyons battling a knock.

Robinson admitted afterwards that there is no chance of longer-term absentees Chris Cadden, Mats Knoester and Lewis Mayo being available, with Dan Happe the only player likely to return for the Saturday night showdown with Premiership bottom dogs Hibs after being suspended last night.

‘Gyamfi will be out for a long period of time,’ reported Robinson. ‘It looked like it was maybe the other thigh that he’s had a problem with. It’s a heartbreaker for the boy.

‘Brad Lyons took a really bad knock. I’m not sure how serious that is. Hopefully, he’s got away with it.

‘Chris Cadden will be out for six weeks. Mats Knoester is four to six weeks.

Emmanuel Gyamfi limped off early with an injury in Aberdeen's semi-final defeat

‘The only plus point is Dan Happe. It’s very difficult because half the squad are not available. Any team that does well, there’s consistency and patience.

‘Over time, the last person that had prolonged success in Aberdeen was Derek (McInnes). He was given a lot of time and a lot of support, but we’ve not been able to put a consistent team, never mind a consistent shape, on the pitch.

‘It’s four changes every week at the moment.’

Robinson, however, stated his pride in the way his players performed and backed centre-back Jack Milne while taking a pop at the criticism he has come in for of late.

‘They rose against adversity this time. They put in a real performance,’ he said.

‘Rangers scored during our best spell in the game, which is unfortunate, but it’s been a tough week for us with injuries, really tough.

‘They showed great character and courage.

‘Jack’s a young man. Surprisingly, he’s from Aberdeen and he still gets an incredible amount of stick.

‘He’s had one full season and he’s a lovely boy that wants to learn. He’s just learning the game. I thought he showed real character.’

Dons boss Stephen Robinson on the touchline in the 1-0 defeat to Rangers

Robinson also refused to criticise captain Kevin Nisbet after seeing him pass up a couple of golden chances.

‘If you want chances to fall to anyone, it’s Kevin and he had the opportunity to play Mitchy Ntelo in with one of them when clean through,’ he reflected.

‘We had grown into the game. We were organised defensively despite not knowing what our team would be until Saturday morning.

‘I’m proud of the players because they gave me absolutely everything.

‘You’d need to ask the fans if they saw positive performances. I can only answer for myself. I saw a group of boys give everything in adversity.

‘Everyone expected us to roll over and get battered but that wasn’t the case. They’ve shown they have the character to play for Aberdeen in big games, no doubt.’

#2156. Farioli a SKY: “Il gol del Napoli ci poteva tagliare le gambe, grande reazione nel secondo tempo”

rancesco Farioli, allenatore del Porto, ha parlato a Sky Sport dopo la vittoria contro il Napoli. “Siamo partiti forte, infatti abbiamo avuto tante occasioni sin dall’inizio. Abbiamo poi sbloccato la gara su palla inattiva e abbiamo avuto parecchie situazioni subito che potevano indirizzare il confronto, ma aver concesso 6-7 minuti a una squadra forte come il Napoli ci ha puniti, il gol subito poteva tagliarci le gambe ma la reazione del secondo tempo è stata fantastica sotto tanti punti di vista, come quello dell’intensità, della determinazione dell’aver concesso poco. Il risultato che arriva dopo tante ottime prestazioni. Avevo chiesto di dare seguito alle prestazioni che avevamo fatto ma con un po’ più di convinzione e fiducia, era nelle piccole cose che si poteva decidere A parte qualche errore, siamo stati bravi ad andare su palla, attaccare a testa bassa e abbiamo fatto tutto che dovevamo per giustificare questa vittoria Bruyne nell’area del Napoli, è servita tanta energia, tanta forza, quando gli azzurri venivano nell’uno contro uno e maggior controllo quando giocavamo nell’area avversaria. Abbiamo interpretato queste tre fasi con grande maturità. Negli ultimi due anni la squadra ha cambiato tanto, ma qui il calcio offensivo e di duelli fa parte del sangue e della storia del Porto: noi abbiamo cercato di riportare quello, di rimetterci quella determinazione e quella forza che ora ci stanno premiando. Sappiamo soffrire, sappiamo essere belli quando servono ma anche umili e lavoratori nei momenti duri: questo mi rende orgoglioso dei ragazzi, ma ora è comunque tempo di pensare al campionato, abbiamo una trasferta complicata”.

#2155. Middlesbrough man to remain sidelined until December

Middlesbrough will remain without the midfielder until the start of December. That means he is set to sit out of their upcoming games against Wrexham, Watford, Bristol City, Swansea City and Southampton.

Pukstas, 22, joined Boro in the summer on a long-term contract from Hadjuk Split running until June 2031. However, he has featured only once for the Championship promotion hopefuls.

In this latest update regarding his condition, his head coach Hellberg has said, as per a report by The Northern Echo: “He’s a month away, maybe, something like that. He’s a strong boy, he came from playing a lot and now he’s doing everything to come back stronger in every way he can. That’s how you try always to look at things, you can work on the things you can work at and try be stronger at that.”

Middlesbrough are sat top of the table after their first 13 fixtures ahead of the likes of Birmingham City, Stoke City, Swansea City and West Brom as they look to claw themselves into the Premier League. They face Wrexham away on Wednesday night.

Middlesbrough signed Pukstas in the last transfer window to bolster their midfield department. He gives them more competition and depth in the middle of the park.

At the age of 22, he has bags of potential and the capacity to grow and develop down the line. In the meantime, he faces a tough battle getting into Hellberg’s starting XI.

The USA youth international has some strong competition for minutes in his position. Boro have the likes of Aidan Morris, Sebastian Berhalter and Riley McGree, among others, at their disposal.

Pukstas started out at Sporting Kansas City before switching to Hadjuk Split as a teenager. He went on to become a key player for the Croatian outfit and scored 27 goals in 138 games before Middlesbrough lured him over to England for a new challenge.

Middlesbrough are having to be patient with Pukstas at the moment. He has struggled with injury since his switch and hasn’t played as much as he would have liked.

He is young though and still has plenty of time left ahead of him at Boro. He can still play a key role when he is back available and fit again as the matches come thick and fast over the December to January period.

Hellberg’s men will be full of confidence at the moment as they aim to keep their momentum going and build a gap at the summit. They beat Charlton Athletic 3-0 at home last time out and will be hoping for something similar against Wrexham next up.

#2154. Why Sebastien Pocognoli picked James Penrice as he talks up Rangers star's quality

Scotland manager Sebastien Pocognoli insists the selection of Rangers' James Penrice was "logical."

The left-back, who has also operated in midfield, has been rewarded for his form so far since moving to Ibrox on loan from AEK Athens. He immediately impressed since joining but was left out of Pocognoli's first squad for the last camp.

Now, the 27-year-old will get the chance to win his first cap when the national team takes on North Macedonia and Switzerland this month.

The Scotland boss says the quality Penrice possesses, and his readiness for his system, made him an obvious choice.

Rangers game rescheduled as a result of Premier Sports Cup final participation WATCH: Nico Raskin turns interviewer after Rangers teammate's Scotland joy

He said: "We have a list of players that we select regarding the performance at their clubs, but also the style of play or the way that we want to play.

"We have some missing players around the left side.

"So, combined with his quality and what we are looking for on the pitch, it's a logical choice for me and it can be very useful for the system that we want to play and also I think he deserves it."

Scotland face North Macedonia at Hampden on November 13 before an away game in Switzerland on November 16.

#2152. Scott Parker’s first West Ham starting XI predicted as Cardiff City clash awaits – Tomas Soucek in

West Ham have acted quickly to replace the sacked Nuno Espirito Santo, naming Scott Parker as their new head coach on Wednesday morning. The former WHUFC captain jumps straight into the action too, as he will lead his new club in the clash with Cardiff City later tonight.

Parker will be backed by a first-team coaching staff including Steve Potts, Gerard Prenderville and Mark Noble, who have overseen preparations for the clash with the Bluebirds.

The new boss will need time to work out his strongest starting XI as he gets to grips with the squad at his disposal. However, his first lineup against Cardiff City could see some welcome reintroductions.

Hermansen; Veltman, Mavropanos, Kilman, Morato; Engels, Soucek; Bowen, Kante, Austbo; Piroe.

Parker has primarily played with a 4-2-3-1 formation over his managerial career to date. It is the system West Ham played in under Nuno too, so for the sake of continuity given the lack of time on the training ground under the new boss, it seems inevitable that they start in this setup against Cardiff City.

The new boss prefers his goalkeepers to be proficient in possession, as shown by James Trafford’s success in his Burnley team. As such, Mads Hermansen should keep his place ahead of Alphonse Areola despite recent questions over the Dane’s form.

Kyle Walker-Peters limped off in the defeat to West Brom, so Joel Veltman should start. Morato continues on the left flank, but in a big boost, Konstantinos Mavropanos may return in place of Airidas Golambeckis as he trained on Monday ahead of the game. If he is not fit though, the youngster will keep his spot.

In midfield, Tomas Soucek replacing Divine Mukasa seems a smart move to give Parker and WHUFC a bit more experience and physicality in midfield. He will partner Arne Engels, and Mohamadou Kante should retain his place in a more advanced roles.

Captain Jarrod Bowen is a shoo-in for a start on the right, and on the left, it’s a toss-up between Edvin Austbo and Manor Solomon. The latter is struggling for form though, so could be benched again.

Up top, Joel Piroe could be the proven Championship goalscorer Parker needs. He has Taty Castellanos and Pablo to pick from too.

It is a rarity to see a head coach appointed and lead their first game on the same day. However, Parker is wasting no time in getting to work with his new West Ham group.

He will lean on his coaching staff for the first game, before likely taking on a more leading role in team selection when WHUFC face Blackburn Rovers on Saturday. Parker will have had more time to assess his players and should have a better idea of who he wants in his preferred lineup.

Cardiff City will pose a tough test, as they look to dominate possession and control their games. The Bluebirds are unbeaten at home, winning their last three on their own patch against Birmingham City, Watford and Charlton Athletic.

Tonight’s game kicks off at 19:45 and will be broadcast live on Sky Sports+.

#2150. Un talento al giorno, David Boly: esordio con gol in Champions, che serata

David Boly, nato il 22 gennaio 2009 a Colombes, è il giovane terzino destro francese (di origini ivoriane) del Paris Saint-Germain. Arrivato nella cantera parigina nel 2022, ha debuttato tra i professionisti in Coupe de France nel dicembre 2025. Nel giro di una settimana ha fatto il suo esordio in Ligue 1 contro il Le Havre e, pochi giorni dopo, ha segnato il suo primo gol in carriera nella vittoria 6-0 in Champions League contro il Villarreal, diventando il secondo più giovane marcatore del PSG nella competizione.

Caratteristiche tecniche Da un punto di vista squisitamente tecnico, è un terzino moderno, atletico e propositivo, che si ispira ad Achraf Hakimi. Si distingue per velocità, capacità di spinta sulla fascia, tecnica pulita e lettura difensiva. Già internazionale francese nelle varie nazionali giovanili, combina fisicità e qualità con la palla, profilandosi come una delle perle più promettenti della generazione dei Titi e del calcio francese.

#2149. Frank McAvennie apologises after criticising Celtic star's grief absence

Former Celtic striker Frank McAvennie has apologised after criticising Auston Trusty for missing games after the death of his father.

The defender went public two weeks ago after sadly losing his dad, paying tribute to him online on October 21.

He sat out games against Celta Vigo, Hibs, Dundee United and Falkirk. In the build-up to the latter, manager Martin O'Neill revealed Trusty was heading back to America to "celebrate his dad's life."

McAvennie was critical of how much football the defender was missing, saying on the Let Me Be Frank podcast: "I've got every respect for that, but how long do you want off? Martin (O'Neill) says he's away to celebrate the life of his dad.

"Celebrate the life? You've got football. You're getting paid and we're in trouble. How long is it going to be?

Former Rangers player to stand trial accused of killing woman in road collision £750,000 padel club with four covered courts to open at Ayr United’s stadium

"It's nonsense. My dad was the best man, the only man I looked up to when he died, but you've got to get on with it."

The comments prompted outrage online, with the pundit accused of being insensitive.

That has prompted an apology in the form of an online statement.

McAvennie said: "I’ve seen some of the reaction to comments I made on the podcast about Auston’s absence from football following the death of his father.

"I can understand why people feel my comments were insensitive, and looking back, I should have chosen my words more carefully.

"Everyone deals with grief differently, and I certainly meant no disrespect to Auston or his family. Celtic need the big man back, and that’s what I was clumsily trying to say.

"I wish him and his family all the very best."

#2148. Jermain Defoe in line for coaching role with 46-year-old set to become Bromley boss

Bromley parted ways with Andy Woodman last month, in a controversial dismissal after five successful years in charge. Woodman had taken the side from the National League to League One, however, they have struggled this season and are bottom of the third tier table.

Byron Webster has been in charge on an interim basis. Now though, reporter Jason Burt states that Karl Robinson is set to join as the Ravens’ new manager.

Robinson has been without a job since June, following his departure from Salford City. He led the side to the League Two play-off final last season, losing out on promotion to Notts County.

Robinson, 46, is set to bring an assistant of his own with him to Hayes Lane, as Burt adds Jermain Defoe is poised to join alongside the new boss. He will serve as no.2 to the experienced manager following a maiden managerial stint of his own at Woking.

At the time of his first managerial appointment, Robinson was aged just 29 with MK Dons – the youngest boss in the EFL at the time. He spent six years with the Dons and has since managed Salford City, Charlton Athletic and Oxford United.

At each of his sides, aside from Charlton Athletic, Robinson has managed over 130 games. He averaged over 1.5 points per game at all of Salford, Oxford and MK, with his Addicks stint being the only exception again at 1.36 PPG.

Robinson often operates with a 3-4-1-2, but his vast experience has seen him switch systems and utilise four-man defences. He likes his sides to have high possession, with wide overloads, aggressive pressing and fluid midfield rotations.

A past shift to a 5-2-1-2 could be repeated at Bromley to provide them with more defensive solidity in their bid to secure League One survival.

In his time as a manager, Robinson has gained most of his experience in League One. He has won 796 points in the division, including 222 wins (Transfermarkt).

Defoe’s experience is more limited, but he is a budding coach determined to forge his way in the dugout after a short spell in charge of Woking. The former England striker has had roles at Tottenham Hotspur and Rangers alongside his stint with the Cardinals, and his experience as a top player could mean he has valuable nuggets of advice to offer to the players.

Bromley have mainly played in a 4-1-4-1 system this season, and it was regularly used last term alongside a 3-4-2-1. They may have to adjust to new demands under Robinson.

Bromley do not have a league fixture until November 21st now, but will hope Robinson can make an impression in cup matches against Rotherham United and MK Dons as he gets to work at Hayes Lane.

Robinson has the experience and tactical nous to bring success at Bromley, but time will tell whether the squad is truly strong enough to achieve such a feat after their quick ascent to League One.

#2147. Farioli in conferenza stampa: “Il Napoli ha preparato la gara in maniera intelligente, match importante”

Francesco Farioli, allenatore del Porto, ha rilasciato alcune dichiarazioni in conferenza stampa dopo la vittoria in Champions League contro il Napoli:  “Il gioco è stato molto pesante, ci sono stati diversi momenti in cui siamo stati molto energetici e coraggiosi, soprattutto quando siamo andati a prendere la palla e loro ci pressavano, avevamo la giusta mentalità come i tifosi e questo ci ha aiutato ad avere la prestazione avuta. In ogni intervista ho detto che è stata una buona Champions ma non avevamo avuto punti, stavolta abbiamo raccolto i frutti. Questa vittoria è importante perchè ci servivano punti, sappiamo la storia del club e l’esigenza di fare bene in Champions League, di ottenere risultati. Una cosa non si può dire, che ci sia mancanza di volontà: ogni tre giorni abbiamo le giuste motivazioni, ci sono sempre gli avversari e le partite da giocare, e non sempre siamo al top della forma e a certi livelli la pressione si fa sentire. Dobbiamo imparare sulla nostra pelle, ci prendiamo i tre punti contro una squadra eccellente e ora vediamo le ultime quattro partite per guadagnare più punti possibili. La partita di stasera e i risultati hanno cambiato la prospettiva del match, abbiamo dovuto cambiare interpretazione del match e il coraggio dei ragazzi è stato notevole. Siamo stati bravissimi nella verticalizzazione e nel secondo tempo, l’impatto delle sostituzioni è stato fondamentale, chi è entrato ha contributo tanto. Cosa non è andato bene alla fine del primo tempo? In Champions ogni punto conta così come ogni gol può fare la differenza, stasera vincere con tre gol di differenza sicuramente è importante e ci aiuta ad avanzare, la performance dei ragazzi è stata eccellente. Le difficoltà del primo tempo? Non sapevamo cosa avrebbero fatto, avevamo preparato cinque scenari possibili e stasera abbiamo visto le scelte degli avversari con giocatori che non ci aspettavamo in campo. Abbiamo reagito bene e fatto la differenza. Come ho visto il Napoli? Non per dare meriti ai miei, ma il Napoli ha preparato la partita in maniera intelligente, quando ho visto la formazione ho pensato come potessero creare. La prima lettura è stata una scelta strategica e ben pensata in pochi minuti, abbiamo cercato di aggiustare le nuove dinamiche con l’ingresso di De Bruyne sotto punta, siamo stati bravi a portare dentro tanta energia, nel secondo tempo siamo venuti fuori con la condizione fisica e ritmo, chi è entrato ha alzato il ritmo ed è qualcosa in cui credo molto. Ho chiesto a Fofana e Gimenez di giocare fuoriruolo ma hanno avuto determinazione e spirito. Il calcio italiano? Le informazioni sono veloci e si può raffrontarsi con altre realtà, la Serie A la seguo da tifoso e da osservatore esterno, alcune dinamiche le conoscete meglio voi. Nuova onda degli allenatori toscani? Facciamo un lavoro bellissimo quando i risultati vanno bene, difficilissimo se la palla non entra. Spalletti ed Allegri sono dei riferimenti del calcio italiano, hanno portato innovazione nel ruolo dell’allenatore e ho un rispetto enorme per ciò che hanno fatto e continuano a fare. Su Alvini posso dire che è incredibile per lo spirito che ha il Frosinone, tanti anni fa andavo a vedere i suoi allenamenti al Tuttocuoio e ha sempre avuto una grande passione. Mi ha aperto le porte del suo centro sportivo per gli allenamenti e ancora non pensavo di allenare, ha un modo contagioso per come allena. Come abbiamo vinto? A fine primo tempo ho detto di avere ancora fiducia come nei primi 35′ di partita. Abbiamo avuto coraggio e alla lunga abbiamo meritato la vittoria, grande meriti ai ragazzi. Gabri Veiga aveva la febbre, aveva una faccia bianca stamattina e non aveva dormito: quando è uscito gli ho detto meglio fare così ogni partita. Ha grande intelligenza, conosce i momenti della gara e oltre a leggere il gioco aggiunge qualcosa di speciale come la sua fisicità strabordante come volume ed esplosività. Ha ancora grandi margini di miglioramento sulla continuità, sta migliorando e rispetto al suo percorso ha voglia di fare tanto e bene. Poi tra cinque anni chissà, per ora ce lo godiamo. Occasione per un italiano all’estero prima di tornare in Italia? A volte non danno e a volte non si prendono le opportunità, ci sono momenti: negli ultimi due anni e mezzo ho lavorato per club con sale piene di trofei. Lavorare assieme come succede al Porto poi ti rende difficile guardare altrove, sono felice qui: è la realtà, sono felice di lavorare con un gruppo straordinario e con le persone che rappresentano il club e fanno di tutto per il club, creativi nel cercare soluzioni nei momenti di difficoltà. Abbiamo tante partite da giocare, testa bassa e pedalare”.

#2145. Scotland Women learn Nations League draw after promotion back to League A

Scotland Women will face Germany in the Nations League after the draw was conducted on Tuesday.

Melissa Andreatta’s side were drawn in Group A4 along with the Germans, the Netherlands and Sweden.

Scotland won promotion back to League A after remaining unbeaten in the Women’s World Cup group stage qualifiers earlier this year.

The Nations League fixtures will begin in February 2027.

Scotland are already set to take on the Swedes in round two of World Cup qualifying in December.

Frank McAvennie apologises after criticising Celtic star's grief absence Former Rangers player to stand trial accused of killing woman in road collision

They also previously faced Germany and the Netherlands in the 2025 Nations League.

Meanwhile, also in League A, European champions England were drawn against France, Austria and Portugal in Group A2.

Wales were pulled out in Group A3 alongside Denmark, Italy and Iceland.

In Group B3, Northern Ireland were drawn out with Poland, Finland and Belarus.

Meanwhile, the Republic of Ireland landed a tough task with Nations League holders Spain in their group along with Norway and Switzerland.

The Nations League will play a key role in determining qualification for both the 2028 Olympic Games in Los Angeles and the 2029 Women's European Championship in Germany.

The winners of League A's four groups will advance to the Nations League Finals in October and automatically secure a place at the expanded Olympic Games. Meanwhile, the team finishing bottom of each League A group will drop down to League B.

Teams ending the campaign third in League A will enter promotion/relegation play-offs against runners-up from League B.

For Northern Ireland, who are competing in League B, topping their group would ensure promotion to League A, while a second-place finish would earn a place in the promotion play-offs.

At the other end of the table, the last-placed team in each League B group will be relegated automatically, along with the two worst-performing third-placed sides.

#2144. Three goals conceded: Ex-Sheffield Wednesday man endures a tough night on UEFA Champions League debut

Eyebrows will have been raised in both Sheffield and Rome when Devis Vasquez was picked to start for AS Roma in their UEFA Champions League clash against Manchester United on Tuesday night. The ex-Sheffield Wednesday loan man has served as backup ‘keeper in Mile Svilar’s absence, but came into the first XI at Old Trafford.

All was going well for former SWFC loan man Vasquez and Roma as they went 2-0 up within 17 minutes. However, the Colombian shot-stopper would concede three second-half goals as United fought back to win 3-2.

Vasquez made two saves but finished with an xG prevented of -0.50. having conceded three times from an xG of 2.50 (Fotmob). He was nearly a hero as he saved a penalty from Bruno Fernandes in the second half, but Roma’s Bryan Cristante was cruelly deemed to have encroached and the Manchester United man fired home the retake.

Vasquez was a 26-year-old goalkeeper on the books of AC Milan when he joined Sheffield Wednesday on loan in 2023. He came in and was the Owls’ starting no.1 under Xisco Munoz, but was dropped from the side after the Spaniard’s sacking and would never play for the club again.

The Long-serving Cameron Dawson stepped back into the starting role, before James Beadle joined in January. Vasquez was then recalled by Milan and promptly loaned out to Ascoli. Almost his entire time with I Rossoneri was spent out on temporary deals, and when he was released in 2025, Roma snapped him up.

Vasquez has served as cover while with Rome and did so in a half-season loan at Besiktas in 2025/26 too. He played once for the Turkish giants, keeping a clean sheet in a 0-0 draw with Fatih Karamguruk back in April.

Barring his loans to SWFC and second-tier Italian sides Ascoli and Empoli, Vasquez has mainly served as a cover option for all of his clubs. The now 28-year-old has the pride of making his UEFA Champions League debut though, despite the result.

Vasquez is looked back on as a fairly minor part of the Sheffield Wednesday side that endured dismal fortunes under Xisco Munoz. He didn’t give a great account of himself with a save percentage of only 59% while at Hillsborough (Fotmob), but the Owls had far bigger problems than their loan goalkeeper at the time as they went winless in their first 10 Championship games that season.

After the Colombian, the Owls had far better fortunes in the goalkeeping department. He was succeeded by loan hit Beadle in January, and the Brighton & Hove Albion prospect would return for the 2024/25 campaign.

The emergence of Pierce Charles would gather pace too, as he cemented himself as one of the UK’s brightest goalkeeping prospects despite shoulder injuries.

Now, as Vasquez earns opportunities with Roma, SWFC have Joe Lumley and Ashley Maynard-Brewer battling for the starting berth. The new ‘keepers will hope to play key roles under new boss Dave Challinor as the club rebuild after administration and relegation.

#2142. Raphinha at the double as Barcelona too strong for Aston Villa

Barcelona ensured Unai Emery had another Nou Camp nightmare as they beat his Aston Villa side 3-0 in the Champions League.

Lamine Yamal’s early opener and a second-half double for Europe’s most prolific scorer Raphinha, who has bagged 19 in 13 games this season, gave Emery a 55th birthday to forget.

The Spaniard will not be keen to return to this city any time soon as he has now lost 15 out of 16 matches at Barca, including the famous ‘Remontada’ nine years ago, when his PSG side lost a last-16 tie 6-1 to squander a 4-0 first-leg lead.

This was supposed to be a Red Letter Day for the club and was watched by super fan Prince William – a reward for their excellent progress in the four years of Emery’s reign.

It might have been more memorable had Villa taken a number of first-half chances to draw level, but defeat ends their 100 per cent start to the league phase.

Frank McAvennie apologises after criticising Celtic star's grief absence £750,000 padel club with four covered courts to open at Ayr United’s stadium

But with some winnable games still to come, they are still a good bet to finish in the top eight.

Barcelona have not found goals hard to come by this season, so the last thing Villa needed to do was hand out a freebie.

But they laid Barca’s opener on a plate in the seventh minute as playing out from a goal-kick went horribly wrong when Joao Gomes blindly passed straight to Yamal, who whipped a finish into the corner from 12 yards.

It was a nightmare start, but they could have been level within minutes as they caused chaos from two corners.

First, Matty Cash arrived late at the far post and his drilled effort was deflected wide and then the second delivery found its way through to Gomes, who thought he was about to make amends until Joao Cancelo produced a fine block.

Villa found their feet and began to expose Barca’s high line. It should have brought an equaliser in the 19th minute as Nicolas Jackson raced clear down the right and teed up Johan Manzambi, who dragged his shot wide.

Then Jackson was sent clear again and it looked like he would beat Joan Garcia to the ball, but the on-rushing Barca goalkeeper got a foot to it and made a vital tackle.

They did suffer another nervy moment when Zion Suzuki put his side in danger again when playing out from the back, but he made up for it by saving Anthony Gordon’s effort.

Villa again threatened as Manzambi surged into the box, but Garcia was equal to it.

Those missed chances proved important as Barca doubled their lead in the 52nd minute. Fermin Lopez fed Raphinha on the edge of the box and his effort deflected off Pau Torres’ heel and into the corner.

The third came 12 minutes later and it was a lovely goal as Cancelo skipped clear of John McGinn and picked out Raphinha, who made no mistake from six yards.

Leon Goretzka sent a glancing header against a post as Villa tried to get on the scoresheet, but the size of the defeat could have got out of hand.

Another poor Suzuki pass played Boubacar Kamara into trouble, with the Japan international denying Fermin from the resulting shot. Yamal, who became the highest-scoring teenager in Champions League history, then whipped two efforts just wide.

#2140. Three goals from 1.27 xG: Sydie Peck defies the numbers to break seven-year Sheffield United duck

Sheffield United continued their fine form on Tuesday night as they defeated Preston North End 3-2 away from home. SUFC are now four games without defeat, and they’ve won four of their last six Championship matches.

It was a strong team performance from the Blades, but the man-of-the-match display from hat-trick hero Sydie Peck can not be overlooked. The 22-year-old midfielder scored three in the first half, becoming the first United player to score a competitive first-team hat-trick since Billy Sharp against Aston Villa in February 2019.

Peck did so from an expected goals (xG) of just 1.27 too. First, his cross bounced all the way through and beyond Daniel Iversen (0.02 xG) before he kept the Preston North End ‘keeper at bay and floated beyond a despairing defence for his second (0.21 xG). Finally, the Blades star slid in at the back post to convert from Romelle Donovan’s cross (0.81 xG) to seal his hat-trick.

Peck registered a total of 1.27 xG across his six shots – the most by anyone in the game. His attacking threat was emphasised by the fact his six touches in the opposition box was more than any other player too.

The Sheffield United man saw to his usual duties. He created two chances in total and completed 52 passes, also winning six out of seven aerial duels – a tally bettered by only Mark McGuinness in the SUFC side (Fotmob).

It was the complete midfield performance from Peck, who has grown in influence this season. His importance creatively is displayed by 21 chances created and 76 line-breaking passes, and 779 touches shows how regularly he is the man to get on the ball when the Blades have possession.

Peck has made 54 defensive contributions too though, winning 52 duels in total. This shows his willingness to get stuck in and do the dirty work, while 50 recoveries highlights his work rate as well (Fotmob).

Now on four goals this season, if Peck can start to score on a more consistent basis, he will really be a complete midfielder for Chris Wilder.

Peck has been with Sheffield United for over five years now, joining the academy after his exit from Arsenal. He broke into the team in the 2024/25 campaign and would grow in the 2025/26 season with five goals and five assists in 41 games across all competitions.

The London-born midfielder drew interest from West Ham in the summer, but he expressed a desire to pay back all those who have shown faith in him at Bramall Lane – including manager Wilder.

Peck is happy with SUFC and his continued improvements this season show his development isn’t being impacted by sticking around either. However, if he continues in his current form, interest is bound to grow, and exit opportunities will become more tempting.

Sheffield United are keen to renew Peck’s contract as his current deal runs out in 2028. It would be wise to strike an agreement sooner rather than later with clubs sure to be alerted by his impressive displays of late.

#2139. Atalanta-Kairat, le probabili formazioni: Sarri a caccia di riscatto

La parola d’ordine in casa nerazzurra è una sola: reagire. Alle ore 18:45, nella cornice della New Balance Arena, l’Atalanta ospita i kazaki del Kairat Almaty per la terza giornata della fase a campionato di Conference League. Una sfida che assume un peso specifico enorme per la squadra di Maurizio Sarri, reduce dal pesante 0-3 casalingo subito contro il Parma in Serie A. Il pesante K.O. di campionato — la quinta sconfitta in dieci gare di Serie A — ha spinto la società a chiedere scusa ai tifosi e a mandare la squadra in ritiro a Zingonia per ritrovare compattezza. In Europa la Dea si trova attualmente al 15° posto con 3 punti, frutto della vittoria per 3-1 all'esordio contro il Pafos e del successivo stop per 2-0 ad Amsterdam contro l'Ajax. Vincere contro il Kairat, fermo a quota 1 punto dopo il pari a Riga (1-1) e la travolgente imbarcata interna contro il Panathinaikos (1-5), è fondamentale per rimettersi in corsa ed evitare le insidie dei playoff.

COME ARRIVA L’ATALANTA - L'Atalanta sta attraversando il momento più complesso della sua stagione: l'ultimo successo risale proprio allo scorso 15 ottobre contro il Pafos e, nelle ultime cinque uscite tra campionato e coppe, ha raccolto due pareggi e tre sconfitte. Per la sfida contro il Kairat, Sarri deve fare i conti con un'infermeria affollata che azzera quasi del tutto le rotazioni, soprattutto nel reparto arretrato e in mediana. Saranno infatti assenti Hien, Kossounou, Pompei, Kessie, Raspadori, Gaetano, Kristensen, Bellanova e Kolasinac. La buona notizia riguarda il rientro tra i convocati di Scamacca, pronto a contendersi una maglia da titolare con Nikola Krstović (autore di 6 gol stagionali), con l'ipotesi di una staffetta a gara in corso. Nel 4-2-3-1 di Sarri, la difesa sarà obbligata: davanti a Carnesecchi giocheranno Scalvini e il giovane Obric al centro, con Bernasconi a sinistra e un ballottaggio a destra tra Zappacosta e il giovane Idele. In mezzo al campo, con Pasalic ed Ederson a gestire i ritmi, la trequarti vedrà agire De Ketelaere in posizione centrale, supportato sugli esterni da Elmas e Zalewski.

COME ARRIVA IL KAIRAT - Il Kairat Almaty sbarca a Bergamo al termine di un periodo di forte transizione. La squadra ha appena chiuso il campionato kazako al secondo posto con 65 punti (a -3 dall'Ordabasy campione) dopo l'1-1 finale contro l'Altay. Un pareggio segnato dalla punizione vincente di Oksanen, ma arrivato dopo le dimissioni del tecnico dei titoli 2024 e 2025, Rafael Urazbakhtin. In panchina seduto ad interim c'è Zhora Arutyunyan, promosso dalla formazione giovanile Kairat-Zhastar. Il percorso europeo dei kazaki è stato finora in salita: la difesa ha mostrato evidenti fragilità concedendo ben sei reti nelle prime due uscite di Conference League. Il pericolo numero uno per i nerazzurri sarà lo spagnolo Marc Gual, autore di 14 gol in 26 presenze stagionali, che agirà da trequartista alle spalle dell'unica punta, il brasiliano Edmilson. Nel 4-2-3-1 di Arutyunyan trova spazio a centrocampo la diga formata da Mendonça e Oksanen, mentre la linea difensiva a quattro vedrà Tapalov, Martynovich, Shirobokov e Mata a protezione del portiere Anarbekov.

#2138. Matheus Cunha scores twice as Manchester United hit Roma with late fightback

Matheus Cunha’s late brace secured Manchester United a jaw-dropping 3-2 comeback win against Roma to breathe new life into their season and ease some of the pressure on Michael Carrick.

A nine-match winless run had left the Red Devils head coach’s job hanging by a thread and Tuesday’s wretched start in the Champions League only pushed him closer to the Old Trafford exit.

United were facing a fourth straight loss after Matias Soule’s avoidable opener was followed up by a Donyell Malen penalty in a first half that ended with boos.

Green and gold balloons had cascaded down the Stretford End as fans protested against the Glazers before the match, with the second half offering a reminder of the special spirit that underpins their club.

His celebration earned him the first of two quick bookings but the relief was as palpable as the delight as United held on for just their third win of the campaign.

#2137. Beukema in mixed zone: “Non possiamo prendere questi gol. Siamo arrabbiati”

“Certo, vogliamo dimostrare questa risposta a tutti, ma anche a noi stessi, perché sappiamo le nostre qualità, abbiamo qualità dentro questo gruppo. #2136. How much West Ham paid to sack Nuno Espirito Santo after missing the chance to do it for free

West Ham made the decision to sack Nuno Espirito Santo on Monday, after a 3-1 home defeat to West Brom. WHUFC have acted quickly to hire Scott Parker as their new boss, but they had to pay a sizeable compensation fee to part ways with Nuno.

The Athletic states that the Hammers paid around £2m to relieve the Portuguese boss of his duties. This is after having the option to do so for nothing after their relegation from the Premier League was confirmed in May.

New manager Parker had the support of board members as early as May, before opting to stick by Nuno. Mark Noble was among the 46-year-old’s strongest backers but Dutch coach John Heitinga was also considered as a potential replacement.

As soon as the decision was made to stick by Nuno in the summer, the pressure was on for the West Ham boss. He failed to keep them in the Premier League, but there was hope he could get them back there having led Wolves to Championship promotion in impressive fashion in 2017/18.

There were encouraging signs, as the Hammers rose as high as top of the table during a run of 10 Championship games unbeaten. They scored for fun too, scoring 30 times in the first 11 matches, including big wins over Wrexham, Lincoln City (both 6-0), Derby County (3-0) and Wolves (4-2).

However, frustrations over the style of football continued with cautious play in possession persisting despite WHUFC’s star power in attack. Defensive concerns persisted too, and when the warning signs led to defeats against Southampton and west Ham, the crowd had turned on Nuno.

United are ultimately made to pay for sticking by Nuno in the summer – £2m to be exact. The appointment of Parker for nothing limits the expense of this managerial change, but when the Portuguese boss could have gone in the summer for nothing, it has proven to be a costly decision by the club.

West Ham’s appointment of Parker has led to a mixed response. Many are pleased to see a former Hammers star and three-time Championship promotion-winning manager join the club, but there are some reservations over his style of play.

Like Nuno, Parker’s tactics in recent jobs have been built on a solid defensive foundation, showing a willingness to let the opposition have the ball, and when his sides do have it, playing conservatively.

However, Parker has adapted his tactics over time. While in charge of Fulham, his side had the second-highest average possession in the Championship with 60.8% (Fotmob), and they went on to win the league. They also recorded the highest xG (90.4 – 20 more than any other team), most big chances created (136) and most touches in the opposition box (1,352).

It will be intriguing to see if Parker reverts to this, or sticks by the defence-first tactics that saw him win promotion with Burnley after conceding only 16 goals in 46 matches.

WHUFC supporters want promotion at any cost, but the criticisms of Nuno’s style of play suggest they would rather brings more forward-thinking and controlling football.

#2135. 14 milioni di euro per il capocannoniere della Champions League. Il miglior colpo dell'ultima estate per soli 6 milioni. Spendere non basta: le incredibili intuizioni del Como sul mercato

Anastasios Douvikas da Atene ha segnato contro il Lipsia, contro il Feyenoord, contro il Manchester United e lo ha rifatto, tre volte, pure contro il Lens. Adesso mette nel mirino Alessandro Del Piero che fece cinque reti consecutive in cinque gare di fila nei primi mesi con la Juventus. Nessun paragone, nessun s'indigni, ma Tasos è una delle tantissime lezioni del Como sul calciomercato. A tutte, che siano le ricchissime di Premier League, le snob di Spagna, le nobili talvolta cadude o decadute d'Italia. Douvikas è stato preso dal Celta Vigo, uno che faceva il titolare ma neanche sempre, era pure in panchina. Sedotto e abbandonato in Spagna, dopo esser stato magistrale pichichi in Olanda ma all'Utrecht. E che dire di Luis Milla: ogni innamorato del pallone, che mastichi tattica e sappia guardare oltre al gol spettacolare o al doppio passo, sa che uno così è un Sacro Graal per un allenatore. Ha trentadue anni e prima d'adesso era stato scartato dalle giovanili dell'Atletico Madrid e del Rayo, aveva girovagato nella provincia spagnola tra Alcorcon, Guijuelo, Fuenlabrada, Tenerife, Granada e Getafe. Con Cesc Fabregas è diventato un giocatore totale, mondiale.

La partita vinta, stravinta, dominata, conquistata ieri in Champions contro il Lens, è stata l'ennesimo manifesto ma mica quello definitivo. Il Como è un capolavoro in evoluzione e in rivoluzione d'idee. In un pallone dove sul mercato fin troppi gravitano attorno alle zone sicure, alle scialuppe di salvataggio, ecco che i lariani osano. Beninteso: lo fanno in una piazza dove non c'è pressione, dove tutto è gloria recente conquistata e allora anche il giovane riesce a performare meglio e con meno angoscia. Però Mirwan Suwarso e Charlie Ludi hanno saputo capire ogni pregio della propria realtà e sublimarla, sfruttando il loro algoritmo nativo del quale già parlavamo in passato. Scegliendo l'allenatore giusto, che sarà uno dei più forti al mondo come Cesc Fabregas, sul quale al momento abbiamo una sola domanda: perché dovrebbe andar via? Perché dovrebbe ascoltare altre sirene? Per andare in progetti senza direzione, senza cognizione di causa, che spendono e spandono e son fatti di troppe teste e zero raziocinio? Il Como è, alla prima volta in Champions, tra le prime otto. Tra le migliori del Mondo. E chissà che potrebbe accadere da qui a due anni...

Il Milan ha pagato 37,9 milioni Christopher Nkunku, il Como ne ha spesi 18 per Milan Baturina. L'Inter ne ha spesi 22,8 per Luis Henrique, il Como 6 per Alex Valle. La Juventus ha pagato 83 milioni per Lois Openda e Randal Kolo Muani, il Como 66 per Nico Paz. La Roma 23,5 milioni di euro per Neil El Aynaoui, il Como 750mila euro per Lucas da Cunha. 20 milioni al Girona dal Napoli per Miguel Gutierrez, 3,5 all'Herta Berlino del Como per Marc-Oliver Kempf. Potremmo andar avanti a lungo, pensiamo a quali colpi il Como non ha azzeccato negli ultimi anni. Alvaro Morata, dite? Sentite cosa dicono i giocatori dello spogliatoio, di lui. "Abbiamo pianto, quando è andato via". C'è stato anche questo, non solo i gol (mancati), nell'era di Alvaro. Non sembri normale quel che ha fatto ieri la formazione di Fabregas. E' un capolavoro destinato a restare, e poco importa se il Lens ha abbandonato la barca al largo dopo un tempo di gara. Abbiamo una grande squadra europea. Occhio...

#2134. Pocognoli reveals plan to get best from returning McTominay, and from Scotland

As Scott McTominay returns to the Scotland squad, a nation expects. The current Tartan Army talisman was back among the list of names selected by Sebastien Pocognoli yesterday after recovering from a heart issue, and then immediately recovering his goalscoring touch with Napoli.

A disappointing World Cup in the summer, both collectively for Scotland and on a personal level for McTominay, is largely being put down in hindsight to the mild benign arrhythmia that left the midfielder struggling in the sweltering summer heat in the States.

“It was much harder,” McTominay explained after the tournament.

“Two days before the first match I felt unwell and didn’t know whether I’d play or not.

“It was tough to prepare myself mentally. Then, the high temperatures made the situation even more difficult. Looking back, it’s a huge regret of my career.”

Pocognoli, interestingly, has another theory about why McTominay never shone in the way that Scotland had hoped in the World Cup, citing a lack of structure in the team. Regardless, with a corrective ablation procedure safely behind him, McTominay is now back and raring to go, having bagged four goals in seven matches since his return to club football.

But his new manager is hoping that his masterplan can eventually lead to Scotland being a team that consistently carries a threat, rather than one that constantly looks to individuals like McTominay to produce moments of magic.

“It was a big reflection for me over the last weeks, to try and reduce the expectation here on two or three players,” Pocognoli said.

“The first thing is, why? When you have players who have made the difference in most of the games through individual actions or a situation from nowhere, it’s normal that people then expect them to do it in every game.

“But for me, I have to find a way of playing to give Robbo [Andy Robertson] an opportunity to score two or three goals like he nearly did in the last camp. Or to give John McGinn an opportunity to score. The strikers, Billy Gilmour - it’s about giving everyone the chance to shine as a collective.

“That will maybe decrease the expectation of one or two players. That’s something I’d like to try to do.

“It’s normal that people expect Scott to always be the best, if that’s what he’s had to deal with in the past. In the World Cup, it was less because the structure around him was less. But expectation was still big on him, it didn’t matter.

“It’s up to me to have a good structure around him so he can shine, but not only him - all of the players.”

All that being said, Pocognoli is as excited as any member of the Tartan Army to see McTominay back in the dark blue, and to see how he will fit into the system that he has implemented since taking the reins.

“I just want to know him a little bit more, from the personal aspect,” he said.

“We all know his quality. I think he can fit into the way we want to play. So, I’m just excited to see him on the pitch, to see if he can bring something extra in the way we want to play.

“Also, to see how he fits into the group with my own eyes. I want to make a real contact with him because before it has only been on the phone or by video call. I’m looking forward to meeting him.

“He’s a big player. He’s a very complete player on the pitch. Scott is good on the ball, he can infiltrate without the ball. He can press high on the pitch or he can be box-to-box.

“It’s a massive boost for us. My job is to combine his quality with other players around him and I think we will.

“I’ve watched most of his games. His first game back was difficult in the last 20 or 30 minutes which was normal. After that, he started to find his way again. He’s now ready. He’s scored goals playing in a similar shape as us at Scotland.

“It was good to see him higher and lower in that shape on the pitch. Is he hungry? Yeah. He was really disappointed to miss the first camp and was already talking about the next camp.

“At that point we weren’t sure because we had to see his rehabilitation. But he was excited to try his best to reach the November camp and he’s still excited now, which is good.

“He will bring experience, quality, an expectation of goals, a presence in the box - and he’ll bring leadership as part of the captain’s group.

“He has a strong voice. He’s a different character to the others but it’s up to me to produce the game plan for him and the players that allows him to shine, and not him to make the team shine, if you know what I mean? That’s the challenge.”

#2133. Lazio, tegola Zaccagni: le due opzioni di Gattuso per sostituire il capitano

Uno stop che non ci voleva. Mattia Zaccagni sarà costretto a rimanere ai box per circa un mese per una lesione muscolare emersa negli esami strumentali di ieri dopo la sostituzione a inizio ripresa contro il Cagliari. "Gli accertamenti hanno evidenziato una lesione del quadricipite femorale sinistro. Il giocatore ha già iniziato il programma terapeutico, verrà monitorato quotidianamente e verrà rivalutato con nuovi esami nei prossimi giorni". Il comunicato della Lazio parla di nuove valutazioni che verranno fatte nei prossimi giorni, ma sicuramente Zaccagni salterà la sfida di domenica contro il Napoli. Uno stop che costringerà Gattuso a fare delle scelte alternative, visto che anche Gudmundsson è ancora ai box e non sarà a disposizione al Maradona. Con Cancellieri ancora non al 100% le opzioni si riducono per il tecnico calabrese.

Ammesso che Cancellieri difficilmente può giocare titolare dopo lo stop che lo ha tenuto lontano dai campi per quasi un mese, Gattuso ha due opzioni. La prima è più "classica" e di fatto riprenderebbe la formazione con cui ha chiuso il match contro il Cagliari. Spostamento di Noslin sulla corsia mancina e ritorno tra i titolari di Andrea Pinamonti dopo oltre un mese al centro dell'attacco. Così facendo però il tecnico calabrese non avrebbe poi soluzioni offensive a gara in corso ad eccezione del già citato Cancellieri. Se Gattuso volesse tenersi un cambio offensivo in panchina, allora potrebbe valutare l'ipotesi "doppio terzino" con Pedraza titolare insieme a Tavares, da capire poi chi giocherebbe come esterno d'attacco. Un'opzione al momento remota che però non è da escludere a priori, con Gattuso che da quest'oggi cercherà di risolvere il rebus fascia sinistra dopo il ko di Zaccagni.

#2132. Three games, three goals, three wins: Midfield maverick stars again as Norwich City thrash Derby County

Norwich City cruised past Derby County with much of the hard work done by half time. NCFC were 3-0 up at the break, and Jovon Makama lashed home a stunning striker to seal the victory in the second half.

It was another strong team performance for the Canaries, as displayed by the fact there were four different goalscorers. However, one man who stood out and got himself on the scoresheet once again was attacking midfielder Paris Maghoma.

Maghoma had Rams midfielder Alex Mowatt biting on every dummy before he fired home from outside the box for the Canaries’ second goal of the night. It made it three goals in three consecutive wins for the maverick playmaker, who looks to be back at his best.

Maghoma made an impression early on in his Norwich City career, scoring in his first starts in the FA Cup and Championship after joining in January. His trickery and flair has frustrated even manager Philippe Clement at times, but it isn’t easy to curtail someone as expressive and skilful as the 25-year-old.

The attacking midfielder was influential in the Derby County win. Alongside his goal, Maghoma created one chance and one big chance, also completed five passes into the final third (Fotmob).

Maghoma had only started two Championship games before his recent three-game scoring and starting run. His per 90 stats show just how impactful he has been when on the pitch though, offering a creative streak and valuable abilities in progressing the ball through passes and dribbling.

Across his 442 league minutes, the NCFC ace is averaging 2.44 chances created, 6.11 line-breaking passes, 57.83 touches and a 60% dribble success rate per 90. His three goals from 10 shots and just 0.87 xG shows how clinical he has been when given an opportunity at goal too – although that does beg the question whether such goalscoring form will continue (all stats via Fotmob).

Maghoma has been a standout player in Norwich City’s three-game winning run, and there will be confidence that he can continue in this vein. However, both he and the entire team face a challenge in the effort to maintain their form.

The last three games have been against Derby County, Watford and Charlton Athletic – three of the league’s worst form teams. If NCFC are to keep pushing up the table and come good on their promotion ambitions, they have to maintain these high standards against tougher opposition.

A trip to Michael Skubala’s Bristol City marks a step up for the Canaries, but it is another winnable match. It would be a real morale boost to enter the upcoming international break off the back of four wins, and Maghoma’s recent form indicates he will have a key part to play in continuing the run.

Published: 06:38 EDT, 2 November 2026 | Updated: 14:53 EDT, 2 November 2026

Arsenal stars and fans were caught by surprise after the club's North London Forever anthem was mistakenly played moments into Remembrance silence before their Women's Super League match with Aston Villa.

Arsenal and Aston Villa had joined the WSL sides paying tribute to the Armed Forces community, past and present, with the fixture having been the Gunners' last women's match at the Emirates before Remembrance Day on November 11.

Players from both sides had gathered by the centre circle, with the stadium announcer then heard requesting fans join in with 'a moment of silence as a mark of respect'.

The silence, however, was unfortunately interrupted when their pre-match anthem The Angel – more commonly known as North London Forever – began playing around the stadium.

Arsenal players, who had bowed their heads for the minute's silence, could be seen quizzically looking at their team-mates as the anthem played.

Players eventually broke away from the centre circle as the anthem continued to play.

Arsenal players lined up for a Remembrance silence, but were visibly surprised when the club's North London Forever anthem played shortly after it began. Source: TikTok - Jodie-woso

Fans commented that they were left confused by the unfortunate interruption to the silence

Shirts from the match, featuring the poppy, are being auctioned in aid of the Royal British Legion

We will remember them. pic.twitter.com/okgfWrEDkQ

The unfortunate moment also caught out fans both in the stadium and watching on television.

'Very disappointed with the apparent mixup with playing north London forever during moment of remembrance, if it was indeed a mistake. All got a bit confusing. Crowd certainly looked confused anyway,' one fan commented.

'Well I’m not convinced North London Forever was supposed to start playing then but okay,' another posted.

'I was watching on tv and I was incredibly confused when NLF started playing, and too make matters worse people were actually singing along,' a third said.

'I was also confused looking around I didn’t know if to sing so I didn’t,' added another.

'As bad as it was, as mentioned it wasn’t going to be a full minutes silence. Also NLF is pre-programmed to play a minute before kick off. Awkward more than anything,' another fan said.

The Gunners shared an image of their players lining up for the tribute on social media, writing 'We will remember them.'

Daily Mail Sport understands Arsenal welcomed members of Islington Veterans Community to Emirates Stadium on Sunday as part of their moment of remembrance.

Arsenal are set to have a full Remembrance commemoration this weekend when their men's team face Hull City on Saturday. The women’s side will also observe a full commemoration at Anfield when they face Liverpool on Sunday.

Arsenal are also among the clubs to have been supporting the Royal British Legion's Poppy Appeal, which has seen poppies woven into their match shirts.

Match shirts are being auctioned on the MatchWornShirt website and app, with net proceeds going directly to the Royal British Legion to support their work with military service members, veterans and their families.

Shirts from Arsenal's WSL fixture against Aston Villa and their Premier League tie at Anfield, which also took place on Sunday, are among the items on auction.

I believe the front players can sometimes compensate for lack of speed [at the back], this is my belief. That is the way I like to coach.

Curtis’s teammate, James Penrice, was also called up by Pocognoli for the first time, and the Belgian explained why he felt now was the right juncture to draft the attacking left-back into his squad.

“We are proactive for each position – two, three or four names. And James is one we have followed closely, because of his qualities also as a wing back.

#2129. Torino, che differenza tra primo e (inizio) secondo tempo: il dato sui granata di Abate

Il Torino di Abate sembra vivere due campionati differenti all'interno della stessa partita. Parliamo di una squadra capace di partire con il piede giusto e mettere in difficoltà gli avversari nei primi 45 minuti, ma anche che, dopo l'intervallo, fatica a ritrovare ritmo e concentrazione, pagando spesso a caro prezzo le difficoltà nella fase iniziale della ripresa. I numeri delle prime dieci giornate di Serie A raccontano chiaramente questa doppia identità: i granata occupano attualmente il decimo posto con 13 punti, un bottino in linea con quello conquistato nella passata stagione. Eppure, osservando il rendimento esclusivamente nei primi tempi, il bilancio sarebbe decisamente più interessante.

Se il campionato terminasse al 45', infatti, Vlasic e compagni avrebbero raccolto 14 punti, appena uno in meno rispetto alla zona europea con un bilancio di di tre vittorie, cinque pareggi e soltanto due sconfitte. Il quadro cambia radicalmente quando si prendono in considerazione i primi 15 minuti dei secondi tempi: in questa speciale classifica, il Toro sarebbe addirittura ultimo insieme al Sassuolo, con appena sette punti conquistati e nessuna vittoria. Un dato preoccupante, che evidenzia una flessione evidente nella concentrazione e nell'atteggiamento della squadra al rientro dagli spogliatoi. E gli esempi non mancano: l’ultimo in ordine di tempo è il gol di Ghedjemis, arrivato al quarto minuto del secondo tempo nella trasferta persa contro il Frosinone. Poi ci sono ancora le reti di Varela contro il Monza e di Pellegrino contro la Fiorentina a confermare una tendenza ormai ricorrente e pure preoccupante.

In vista della sfida contro il Lecce, Abate sarà chiamato a intervenire proprio su questo aspetto, l’approccio ai secondi. Perché il Toro ha dimostrato di avere basi e prospettive interessanti, ma adesso deve confermarle per tutti i novanta minuti e non soltanto a corrente alternata.

#2128. Nathan Jones confirms worry over ‘tough’ injury setback for Charlton Athletic summer signing

Nathaniel Chalobah made his first start for Charlton Athletic since the end of August on Tuesday night. However, he only lasted until half time following a crunching challenge from Akin Famewo.

And, unfortunately for CAFC, it appears that the tackle aggravated the thigh injury that kept Chalobah out for two months. Speaking to South London Sport, Nathan Jones said: “He got injured. I think it is the same thing as what kept him out at the start of the season – his thigh.

“We will have the diagnosis, but it wasn’t a tactical decision, we won every game with him at the start of the season and then he got injured. We tried to bring him back – he has trained with us for nearly two weeks so it is not too early. We were always going to give him 55-60 minutes anyway, but I think he has pulled his thigh. It is a tough one again.“

Chalobah’s last start came against Preston North End, a game Charlton Athletic won 1-0. The Addicks won their first three games of the season with the former Chelsea and Sheffield Wednesday man in midfield, but they have not won since.

Chalobah has gained a wealth of experience in the Championship, but he has often struggled with injuries. Previous club Sheffield Wednesday spent large spells without the 31-year-old, and the same fate looks to have hit the Addicks. He played only 31 times in a two-year stay at Hillsborough.

Chalobah’s most notable injury came during his time at Watford, when he had major knee surgery in 2018 and spent a total of 340 days sidelined.

Fears are that his injury struggles are set to persist at CAFC. His influence is clear to see as a strong defensive presence in the middle of the park, but the likes of Conor Coventry, Oliver Skipp and potentially the rarely-seen Timothy Ouma will have to cover in his absence.

In the summer, Charlton Athletic recruited some experienced Championship including midfielders Chalobah and Skipp. However, the former is dealing with injuries, and the latter is yet to win a game in CAFC colours.

They bolstered their forward line with Millenic Alli and Karlan Grant. Grant has scored two goals this season, however, Alli is yet to score and both are being outshone by academy graduate Tyreece Campbell – who has not scored in the last five league matches himself.

The Addicks need more from their summer signings, with disappointment over Chalobah’s lack of fitness and the limited impacts of bigger names like Grant, Skipp and more.

Charlton Athletic have some tough matches on the horizon, with Sheffield United, Wolves, Lincoln City and Blackburn Rovers to play before the end of the month. Time will tell just how long Chalobah faces out, but Jones has to find a winning formula with his available players, otherwise his future at The Valley will be in doubt as their winless run stretches to 11.
